**Observed (2026-11-19, branch `feat/lang-v2-f3s1-schedule`, `taskset -c 0-5`).** Red, green, red again, one group at a time. Red first: the module as a stub that returns wrong answers (group 1, scenarios 1 to 8: 9 of 9 failed; group 2: 8 of 18, and the kind test with the old `series.mjs` `'none' !== 'done'`; group 3: 8 of 26; group 4: 5 of 31). Green: 31 of 31 in the file, with the code-shape and barrier tests 167 of 167. Red again under 49 one-line mutations of the code each scenario guards, every one red in the tests it belongs to; examples: the mask parity turned (`'8-13' !== '0-5'`, task 04 round 1: C#), the order made the task id (`round 1 is sorted by the sha256 of the seed, round and task`, and twenty seeds gave 1 distinct order), the round rule switched off (the barrier sentence `slot 6 (round 1, task 06) is not finished ...` where `round 2 never starts while a pair of round 1 is unscored` was asked), the read-back gate switched off (4 tests red), the refuted line not applied (2 red), the stop check, the one-stack check, the A/A repeat limit and the twin masks each red in their own test. Two mutants survived the first writing (the row `G-1` and the verdict word `holds` could be dropped without a red); the test of scenario 15e was split so that a line of another row and a line with another verdict word each stand alone, and both went red. One test was vacuous on the stub (it passed with zero slots) and now asserts 30 slots first. The whole lang-explore suite (`node .agents/conventions/tools/low-priority.mjs taskset -c 0-5 node --test --test-concurrency=1 spikes/lang-explore/test/`, 2026-11-19): plain 1065 tests, 1058 passed, 5 failed, 2 skipped, 577 s; with `BENCH_REQUIRE_SANDBOX=1` 1065 tests, 1056 passed, 8 failed, 1 skipped, 561 s. The same five fail on a detached `origin/main` worktree, plain and with the sandbox flag, in `e2e.test.mjs` and `olympiad-rehearse.test.mjs` (the preflight refuses: `the box must hold exactly one SDK (2 listed)`, the host's box has two): `A_pair_runs_through_the_pilots_loop_...`, `A_stub_pair_past_the_pause_point_...`, `F2_a_pair_halted_by_the_quiet_check_...`, `The_rehearsal_runs_a_pair_end_to_end_for_free_and_every_criterion_holds`, and the same for tasks 02, 03 and 09. The three extra failures of the sandbox run were environmental and are not the change's: `The_frozen_spec_blobs_at_the_freeze_commit_...` needs `LANG_V2_TASK_REPO` (green, 9 of 9 in its file, with it set to the task repository's checkout), and two e2e tests found the plan lock `series.lock` held by another process's run (`A_pair_that_cannot_start_...`, `F2_a_session_left_launching_...`; green in the file's rerun, which then showed main's five only). The code-shape tests pass with the new module (every function under 50 lines and a complexity of at most 4).

**Plan round 2 (same branch, `taskset -c 0-5`).** Red first on the code as committed: 11 of 36 failed (the offers had no `todo`, the old pilot refusals); green 36 of 36 (172 of 172 with the code-shape and barrier tests); red again under 13 mutations, each red where it belongs: the step table swapped (2), the one-stack refusal off (3), the resume list widened to scored sessions (2), the line looked up by attempt 1's rid (3), a void attempt made to wait for a line (2), a refuted line applied by any rid (5), the re-run offered for both stacks (7), the stop check off (2), and one per pilot refusal (the web task widened, the driver tasks widened, the duplicate allowed, the stack check off, the required-field check off: 1 each). A whole plain run after this round (4.7 GB available at its start): 1070 tests, 1063 passed, 5 failed, 2 skipped, 613 s; the five are the same `e2e.test.mjs` and `olympiad-rehearse.test.mjs` failures as on `origin/main` (the box holds two SDKs). The sandbox-flag run below was made before this round and was not repeated.

**Plan round 2, second pass (same branch, `taskset -c 0-5`).** Red first on the code as committed: 3 of 41 failed (the A/A half-run refusal, the conflicting read-back line, `slotProblem` on a conflict); the pilot-pair table, the kind test and the rest of the `slotProblem` rows were green already and were proved by mutation. Five of the failures are the known ones of `e2e.test.mjs` and `olympiad-rehearse.test.mjs` (the preflight finds two SDKs); the sixth, `On_the_certifying_host_a_case_scope_reads_memory_max_...` in `quick-memory-real.test.mjs`, depends on the host's free memory: it failed in the plain whole run (214 s) and again alone on this branch and, on a detached worktree of the branch's base `8cd4f085`, alone as well, and passed once on that base before. v1's suite (`node spikes/minigit-bench/run-tests.mjs`, the change touches `bare-plan.mjs`): plain 1260 tests, 1256 passed, 4 failed, 484 s; with `BENCH_REQUIRE_SANDBOX=1` 1260 tests, 1257 passed, 3 failed, 491 s. **Code round 2 (coai `proceed`, 5 findings; same branch after the merge of `origin/main`, `taskset -c 0-5`, targeted tests only: whole suites were running elsewhere).** Red first: 4 of 53 failed (a crash between planning the two sessions of a slot, a pilot pair and the A/A, which `halfProblem` refused for ever; impossible read-back dates such as `2026-02-30` and `2026-99-99` counting as dates; `pilotPlan()` throwing a TypeError); the two refactors (the rid array built without `push`, `TASKS` derived from `FREEZE_TASKS`) were green already. The offer for a lone planned session names both sessions, the lone one (`launch`) and the missing one (`plan`), so the two stacks start together. No whole suite was run in this round.

**Code round 2, the observed red messages** (reproduced 2026-11-20 by putting the library of `7323abec` and the tests of `8c2fd3ee` side by side in the worktree, `node .agents/conventions/tools/low-priority.mjs taskset -c 0-5 node --test spikes/lang-explore/test/stage-schedule.test.mjs`: 53 tests, 49 pass, 4 fail; the files were put back afterwards): (1) `A_crash_between_planning_the_two_sessions_of_a_slot_...`: `AssertionError [ERR_ASSERTION]: slot 1 (round 1, task 08) holds sessions of one stack only (node: none, csharp: open): the schedule plans both stacks together`, `false !== true` (the refusal for ever). (2) `A_crash_between_planning_the_two_sessions_of_a_pilot_pair_or_the_A_A_...`: `AssertionError: Expected values to be strictly deep-equal`, actual `[false, undefined, undefined, null]` against expected `[true, 'pilot:05', 'resume', [['csharp', 1, 'launch'], ['node', 1, 'plan']]]` (the pair was refused). (3) `A_read_back_date_must_be_a_real_calendar_date`: `AssertionError [ERR_ASSERTION]: 2026-99-99 is ignored like an undated line: the gate stays closed`, `true !== false` (the gate opened on an impossible date). (4) `pilotPlan_and_pilotStep_with_no_argument_fail_with_a_sentence_and_never_throw`: `TypeError [Error]: Cannot destructure property 'pilot' of 'undefined' as it is undefined.`

**Code round 3 (coai `proceed`, 5 findings; targeted tests only, every run through `node .agents/conventions/tools/low-priority.mjs taskset -c 0-5 node`).** The partner's `plan` comes BEFORE the lone session's `launch` in the recovery offer of a slot, a pilot pair and the A/A (todo[0] is the plan of the missing stack or twin), so a second interruption leaves one planned or both planned, never one launched without its partner. Red first (the tests written first, 53 tests, 51 pass, 2 fail), the messages printed: (1) `A_crash_between_planning_the_two_sessions_of_a_slot_...`: `AssertionError [ERR_ASSERTION]: the missing stack is planned FIRST and the planned session launched after it: a second interruption leaves one planned or both planned, never one launched without its partner`, the diff showing the actual todo starting `['csharp', 1, '5ef225', 'launch']` where `['node', 1, <rid>, 'plan']` was expected; (2) `A_crash_between_planning_the_two_sessions_of_a_pilot_pair_or_the_A_A_...`: `AssertionError [ERR_ASSERTION]: Expected values to be strictly deep-equal`, actual `[true, 'pilot:05', 'resume', [['csharp', 1, 'launch'], ...` where `[['node', 1, 'plan'], ['csharp', 1, 'launch']]` was expected. Green: 53 of 53, 189 of 189 with the code-shape and barrier tests. Red again under 2 mutations (the lone session first in a slot or pilot pair: 2 tests red; first for an A/A twin: 1 test red).

**What this does not prove.** The schedule is a pure function over a run-map as the tests build it; that the real run-map carries the fields it reads (`kind`, `pair`, `task`, `twin`) is PR 3's to meet, and no paid launch, scoring or ledger line is involved. `TASK_KINDS` is a copy held equal to the pins by a test, not read from them.

## F3.S1 (a), bench PR 3: the Stage 1 series over six tasks (`spikes/lang-explore/`, one seam in `spikes/minigit-bench/`)

Branch `feat/lang-v2-f3s1-series`, cut from PR 2's branch (merged to `main` as #236). Nothing here launches a paid session or calls a real model: the tests use fakes (the fake loop of `test/stage-run-fixture.mjs` for `io.runSeams.loopFn`, v1's test rig `bare-loop-harness.mjs` for the real loop, fixture held-out cases, a scripted quiet machine) and the rehearsal uses v1's stub CLI and stub upstream with a fake token. Every run goes through the mount's wrapper.

node .agents/conventions/tools/low-priority.mjs taskset -c 0-5 node --test spikes/minigit-bench/test/explore-seams.test.mjs spikes/minigit-bench/test/bare-series.test.mjs
```

### coai code round 2 (PR 3, 4 accepted findings; each new test observed red, green, red again, 2026-11-20)

**Free rehearsal of round 1** (`stage-init --rehearsal` then `stage-run --what next` for slot 1 and `--what next --until round` for slots 2 to 6; series `r-20261119-3b67`, the stub CLI, fixture held-out cases, the task repository checked out at W in a scratch clone): all six slots ran end to end, 12 sessions, every one `scored`, every record complete (`missingPaths(record, scored, stage)` empty, arm `bare`, model `claude-sonnet-5-5`, each task and round 1), ledger lines of kind `data` of $0.0092 each ($0.1108 in all, the stub's own figure). Wall: slot 1 5 min 34 s; slots 2 to 6 25 min 20 s. `stage-status` printed the six slots finished and the five spend lines. The rehearsal's scoring is one pass (no `build.json`), so `storedProblems` lists `scoring.runSh` for those sessions; the ten-pass path writes it (`stage-facts.test.mjs`).

**Whole runs** (at the head that merges, `taskset -c 0-5`, `--test-concurrency=1`): lang-explore plain 1162 tests, 1160 pass, 0 fail, 2 skipped, 12 min 47 s; with `BENCH_REQUIRE_SANDBOX=1 LANG_V2_TASK_REPO=<task repository checkout>` 1162 tests, 1161 pass, 0 fail, 1 skipped, 12 min 57 s (the skip in both is `The_whole_leak_check_over_5_million_case_lines_...`, off unless `LEAK_PERF=1`; the plain run's second skip is a case that needs the task repository, which the sandbox run has); v1 `node spikes/minigit-bench/run-tests.mjs` 1318 tests, 1318 pass, 0 skipped, 7 min 15 s (the first v1 whole run found two defects of this PR, both fixed and re-run whole: `stage-facts.mjs` wrote bwrap flags as literals, which `box-builders-scan.test.mjs` refuses (the binds are now read through v1's `bindPairsOf`), and `planItems` of `bare-series.mjs` went over complexity 4 (`ownsItems`)). PR 2's head was 1096 for lang-explore and 1317 for v1; this PR adds 66 and 1.

**What this does not prove.** The loop-level scenarios run over v1's rig and the fake loop, so a real stage-run of a ten-pass scoring under the real guard is not exercised here (the guards are PR 4's and PR 5's; until they merge a paid series cannot be made or run). The A/A and pilot units run in the loop tests and the fake-loop tests, not in a rehearsal. The scoring order inside a unit follows the plan's order (the mutation that removed the sort was not caught: the leading stack is already first).

### PR 4 coai code round 1 (2026-11-20, branch `feat/lang-v2-f3s1-guards` from `1859bace`)

Seven findings accepted by coai, each with RED-GREEN-RED: the test first against the code of `HEAD` (red), the fix (green), then the old code put back as one mutation (red again), the fix restored from a copy. ### PR 4 coai code round 3 (2026-11-20, four findings and a link audit, head `8c63acb9`)

The symlink class came back a third time, so this round starts with an audit of every site of `stage-archive.mjs`, `stage-list.mjs`, `stage-progress.mjs`, `stage-disk.mjs` and `stage-heldout.mjs` (and the helpers they call to write) that creates, writes, renames, chmods or removes a file or folder. The fix is ONE helper, `lib/safe-place.mjs`: `linkOnWay` (an `lstat` of every folder on the way from a root; the root only when asked), `placeFile` and `placeLink` (refuse a link on the way before any folder is made; write an exclusive temporary file, a link at that name removed first, `wx` or `COPYFILE_EXCL`; `rename` over the target, so a link at the target is replaced, never followed). Round 2's `linkInCopyRoot` now calls `linkOnWay`; nothing is repeated.

| Site | What it does | Can a planted link make it act outside? | Now |
|---|---|---|---|
| `archive` `copyEntry` (the copy under `<to>/<series>`) | mkdir, remove target, symlink, temporary copy, chmod, rename | a link at a parent: yes (round 2 pre-pass only); at the temporary name: yes (fixed in round 2); at the target: no (replaced) | `placeFile` / `placeLink` with the root checked; the whole-archive pre-pass `linkInCopyRoot` stays (it uses `linkOnWay`). Tests: round 2's two R1 tests |
| `recordArchive` the hash list `<dest>.<12 hex>.sha256list` in the backup | `writeFileSync` | YES: a link at the predictable name was followed and the file behind it truncated | `placeFile` (exclusive temporary file, rename). Test: `The_hash_list_is_written_through_an_exclusive_temporary_file...` |
| `recordArchive` `archive.json` (`writeJson`) in the series | temporary file `archive.json.<pid>.tmp`, rename | YES at the temporary name (`writeFileSync` followed it) | `placeFile`. Test: `archive_json_and_freed_json_are_written_through...` |
| `writeFreed` `freed.json` in `s/<rid>/` | the same `writeJson` | YES at the temporary name; YES when `s` or `s/<rid>` is a link (the file lands outside) | `placeFile` with root = the series (looks at `s` and `s/<rid>`); a refusal keeps the session whole and names it (`keptWhole`). Tests: the same test; `A_session_folder_that_is_a_link_is_never_freed_into...` |
| `removeFiles` (chmod of the roots, `rmSync` of listed files, `rmdirSync` of empty folders) | chmod / remove under the three cache roots of a session | a link at a root or on the way: was YES (round Opus O2) | already fixed: `plainRoots` keeps and names a root that is, or lies under, a link; inside a root `chmodTree` skips links, `rmSync` removes a link and not what it points at, `subFolders` lists real folders only. Unchanged; test: O2 |
| `restore` `placeItem` | mkdir, remove, symlink, `copyFileSync`, chmod, rename into the session | YES: a link at a parent (such as `box/npm`) and at the temporary name were followed | `placeFile` / `placeLink` with root = the series, and `restoreLinkProblem` looks at `s/<rid>` and every folder before ANYTHING is placed. Tests: `A_restore_refuses_a_link_planted...`, `A_restore_replaces_a_link_planted_at_a_temporary_name_or_at_a_target...` |
| `restoreSession` removal of `freed.json` | `rmSync` | YES when `s/<rid>` is a link (it removed a file behind the link) | `restoreLinkProblem` runs first. Test: `A_freed_session_folder_that_became_a_link_is_not_restored_into...` |
| `diskGate` the halt's event line (`appendLedger`) | `appendFileSync` on `ledger.ndjson` | YES for a link at the ledger (append follows it). `appendLedger` is shared with every spend line and part of the frozen harness, so it is not changed; this site looks first | `recordHalt`: a ledger that is a link is a refusal (exit 4) and no line is written. Limit: every other verb still appends through `appendLedger`. Test: `A_halt_does_not_append_its_event_line_through_a_link...` |
| `withSeriesLock` / `acquireSeriesLock` (the lock file in the series) | `openSync(file, 'wx')`, `rmSync` of its own lock | no: `wx` never follows a link and refuses an existing name | none |
| `stage-heldout` (no write of its own) through `withOpenedHeldout` | an owned temporary folder inside the private `heldout-NN/`, a rename of `cases/` after an `lstat` of its absence, the stamp with `wx`, a removal by rename and a delete of an owned folder | no: random owned names with an exclusive claim, an `lstat` before the rename, `wx` for the stamp; the folder is the operator's private 700 area, hardened in PR 4a rounds 1 and 2 (a link in `cases/` or in the backup's place is refused) | none |
| `stage-list`, `stage-progress` | read only (`lstat`, `readdir`, hashing) and a callback | no write | none |
| reads of the archived copy (`copyOf`, `hashAt`, `unproven`) | read and hash | a link in the backup could point at other bytes, but every byte is checked against the listed sha256 before it is placed or trusted | none |

```

**Files run alone after the last commit (coai code round 3)** (`taskset -c 0-5 node --test --test-concurrency=1`, 2026-11-20; pass, 0 fail each): `stage-amendments` 9, `stage-archive` 29, `stage-commands` 12, `stage-disk` 12, `stage-facts` 3, `stage-guard-real` 3 (2 pass, 1 skipped), `stage-guard` 19, `stage-heldout` 7, `stage-loop` 8, `stage-recovery-commands` 9, `stage-rounds` 16, `stage-run` 19, `stage-schedule` 53, `stage-series` 8, `stage-wiring` 10, `commands` 15, `code-shape` 157, `quick-freeze-workspaces` 15, `quick-freeze-record` 1, `quick-freeze-consumers` 4, `quick-workspace` 9 (8 pass, 1 skipped), `quick-heldout-sealed-readers` 8, `quick-heldout-entry-points` 14, `quick-run-harness-gate` 3, `workspace` 8; lang-bench `spec-freeze-record` 27.

**What this does not prove.** The wiring tests run the fake loop (`io.runSeams.loopFn`), so no real held-out folder is made, no real probe runs inside `stage-run`, and the disk gate reads a fake free-bytes function and a fake floor; each real half is tested alone (`stage-heldout.test.mjs` with invented keys, 59 with the layer). The default `io.stage.floorBytes` is read from the pre-registration's manifest, which is PR 6's file and does not exist yet: until then a paid launch is refused by the guard first and by the missing size second. A rehearsal now runs the probe once per unit; with `--until round` that is one empty program per slot.

**Files run alone** (each file on its own, `taskset -c 0-5 node --test --test-concurrency=1`, 2026-11-20, at the head of this PR; the whole suites are the team lead's): `stage-amendments` 9, `stage-archive` 8, `stage-commands` 12, `stage-disk` 7, `stage-facts` 3, `stage-guard-real` 3 (2 pass, 1 skipped: no `LANG_V2_TASK_REPO`), `stage-guard` 12, `stage-heldout` 4, `stage-loop` 8, `stage-recovery-commands` 5, `stage-rounds` 16, `stage-run` 19, `stage-schedule` 53, `stage-series` 8, `stage-wiring` 7, `commands` 15, `code-shape` 155 (the new `stage-launch.mjs` is in it; `stage-step.mjs`'s `runChosenUnit` was cut after the first run went over complexity 4) pass, 0 fail; and every file that imports `explore.mjs`: `note` 10, `quick-package-seal` 3, `prepare` 8, `quick-heldout-round1` 17, `quick-heldout-round2` 6, `quick-package-command` 18, `quick-package-registry` 20, `quick-leak-candidate-baseline` 7, `network` 2, `olympiad-rehearse` 2 (136 s), `olympiad-note` 8, `e2e` 5 (132 s), `quick-freeze-workspaces` 15, `quick-heldout-entry-points` 14, `quick-leak-no-text` 4, `olympiad-tasks` 18, `quick-heldout-verbs` 7, `plan` 6, `quick-note-verdicts` 2, `quick-memory-value` 8, `quick-package-real` 5 (204 s), `quick-repartition` 9 pass, 0 fail; lang-bench (`blobAtTag` is exported): `spec-freeze` 38, `spec-freeze-record` 27, `spec-freeze-order` 1, `spec-freeze-pins` 5 (4 pass, 1 skipped), `code-shape-freeze` 15 pass, 0 fail. `stage-guard-real` with `BENCH_REQUIRE_SANDBOX=1` and no task repository fails its one case that needs one, as it must.

## F2.S2 (b): the task repository's rulesets (`spikes/lang-bench/`)

The two files `test/github-lang.test.mjs` (the library: the data, the pure compare, the reads, the apply, `scrub`, the prerequisites) and `test/github-rulesets.test.mjs` (the command: usage, `--check`, `--apply`, the real process) run over a **fake GitHub**
(`test/support/github-fixtures.mjs`: the `runBounded` seam answered from memory, recording every call) and, for the real-process cases, over a `gh` shell shim that the test writes into an owned folder and puts first on the child's `PATH`. No test
writes to GitHub and none touches the network; the last test of each file proves it from the recorded calls (every call named `gh api`, and the only write any test made was a POST to a fake). The real apply is **not** a test: it is the team lead's, at freeze
step (e) 3, once all six packages pass and a dry run is clean.

```bash
node spikes/lang-bench/run-tests.mjs test/github-lang.test.mjs test/github-rulesets.test.mjs
```

| Scenarios | What they prove |
|---|---|
| The data: `Main_restricts_no_update_and_requires_linear_history`, `Main_has_no_required_status_checks_in_stage_1`, the two tag rulesets and their one bypass, `The_data_is_three_frozen_rulesets_...` (three GitHub rulesets are the owner's two approvals) | the rulesets are exactly what the owner approved, frozen, with no `update` rule on `main`, no bypass but the admin role on creation |
| The compare: a read-back equal passes and what GitHub adds is ignored; one scenario per kind of difference (a missing or extra rule, bypass actor, ref, exclusion, enforcement, target, parameter) | a difference is named in a fixed sentence, and a nothing-read is not equal |
| The reads: list then each by id as argv with no shell and a timeout; a gh that fails, times out, is truncated or prints garbage is a failure to read; a paged or non-list answer and a bad repository name are refused | no failure to read is ever an empty list or an `equal` |
| The apply: an absent ruleset is created and read back; a present equal one is left alone; only the absent one is created; a present different one (or two of one name) is refused before any write; a read-back that differs or is absent is a failure; a stranger is named and left alone | it only creates, never updates or deletes, and reads back |
| **The prerequisites** (`An_early_apply_makes_no_gh_call_at_all_while_a_package_is_missing` and the other gate tests, the pure `The_gate_...` tests, `The_real_command_refuses_an_early_apply_before_it_starts_gh`) | `--apply` makes no `gh` call, not even a read, while `packages.json` shows no passed package (identity and sealed archive of 64 hex, both references `accepted`; `{}`, a missing archive or a reference not accepted each refuse) for any of the six tasks 04, 05, 06, 07, 08, 10, or the `--dry-run-record` file's first line is not exactly `dry run clean <yyyy-mm-dd> packages <sha256 of packages.json>`, or that sha256 is not the one of the file the command read (`A_task_entry_must_show_a_passed_package_not_just_be_an_object`, `The_dry_run_record_is_bound_to_the_registry_by_the_sha256_of_its_bytes`, and the two command tests `An_apply_whose_task_entries_do_not_show_a_passed_package_makes_no_gh_call`, `A_dry_run_record_made_against_another_registry_makes_no_gh_call_and_the_matching_one_applies`) |
| **The partial apply** (`A_failed_create_says_which_are_confirmed_created_which_uncertain_and_which_not_attempted`, `A_second_apply_after_a_transient_failure_creates_only_the_rulesets_still_absent`, a lost-answer rerun, `Each_create_is_read_back_before_the_next_one_is_made`, a wrong read-back stops the next create, the command's exit 3 and its rerun) | a stop reports the three lists, and a rerun creates only what is still absent and never twice |
| **Code rounds** (`A_read_back_without_its_bypass_actors_is_a_problem_never_no_bypass_actors`, `An_apply_without_the_go_refuses_before_its_first_read`, `A_stderr_longer_than_the_slice_is_scrubbed_before_it_is_cut`, `The_read_back_after_a_create_refuses_a_list_the_first_read_would_refuse`, `A_name_is_never_both_confirmed_and_uncertain_and_an_untouched_ruleset_that_changed_is_said_so`, `One_progress_line_goes_to_stderr_before_the_first_read_and_stdout_stays_the_result`, `The_default_packages_file_is_the_registry_of_the_packages`, the shim marker file) | a bypass list left out by GitHub is never `equal`; the library itself holds the go; no token prefix survives the cut; one shared list check; the stop message's three lists never repeat a name; the shim tests prove the shim ran (or, for a refusal, did not) |
| **Code round 3** (`The_include_and_exclude_refs_are_compared_exactly_with_the_data`, `A_ref_wording_that_finds_nothing_still_names_the_exact_difference`, `Only_the_task_repository_is_read_or_written_and_any_other_repository_makes_no_call`; the fixtures build new objects with the difference, no `.pop()` or assignment into a fixture) | a narrowed, widened or extra ref is a problem and exact refs are equal; another valid owner/name makes no call, in the reads and in the apply |
| **Closing round** (`A_dry_run_record_flag_followed_by_nothing_or_by_another_flag_is_a_usage_error_naming_the_missing_value`, `A_stop_after_a_ruleset_that_reads_back_different_asks_for_a_review_and_not_for_a_rerun`, `Every_other_stop_keeps_the_rerun_advice`) | `--dry-run-record` followed by nothing or another flag is exit 2 naming the missing file name (no call); a stored ruleset that differs gets the review-and-owner's-go wording, every other stop keeps the rerun advice |
| **The repository is data** (`The_repository_is_fixed_in_the_data_and_no_flag_names_another`, `The_usage_text_lists_every_accepted_flag_and_the_dry_run_line`) | no `--repo` in any spelling; exit 2 with no call; every call goes to `TASK_REPO` |
| `A_token_in_gits_words_is_removed_from_every_reason`, `The_environment_of_gh_carries_no_token_and_the_PAT_file_is_never_read`, `The_real_command_is_exit_3_when_gh_fails_and_prints_no_token` | `scrub` removes a token everywhere; the child's environment carries no token variable; the library has no file read |
| `A_check_...`, `Merge_settings_are_reported_but_never_change_the_exit`, the exit-code scenarios, `The_real_command_reads_through_a_gh_on_its_path_...` | exit 0 equal, 1 different or absent, 2 usage, 3 gh failed, in process and in a real process |

**The token scan these tests sit under** (`test/support/token-scan.mjs`, run over every spike file by `No_test_binds_a_token_other_than_bench_and_the_scan_finds_a_planted_one_in_every_form` in `test/sweep.test.mjs`). The repository has one registered owned-name token, `bench`; `foreignTokens` finds every
binding of another: a `token:` value that is not the literal `'bench'`, the shorthand `{ token }`, a `TOKEN`-style constant (`const TOKEN = ...`, `OWNED_TOKEN = ...`, `V2_TOKEN = ...`; the name may hold digits) other than `'bench'`, and any value after `--owned-token`. **An assignment to an environment variable is not a
binding:** `process.env.GH_TOKEN = ...` (the exemption is anchored on a word boundary, so `notprocess.env.OWNED_TOKEN = x` is still found; and the bracket form `process.env['GH_TOKEN'] = ...`, which never matched) sets a variable that a child process reads, so it is skipped; the token-named variable of `github-lang.test.mjs` is the case. The tests
`An_assignment_to_an_environment_variable_is_no_token_binding_and_every_real_binding_beside_it_is_still_found` and `The_environment_exemption_is_anchored_and_a_TOKEN_constant_name_may_hold_digits` hold both halves: every spelling of an environment assignment is clean, and each real form beside them is still found.

**Not covered, and why.** The real GitHub: the rulesets' effect (a blocked push, an admin's tag creation) is checked by the team lead's hand `--check` after the apply and by the freeze's own steps, never by a test, because a test must not write to GitHub.
The admin role's id (5) is the integrations provider's documented value; whether GitHub accepts it is known only at the apply, whose read-back would show a refusal as exit 3.

## F3.S1 (b), bench PR 5: the pre-registration tools, the guard over Stage 1's lists and the wiring into `stage-run` and `stage-init` (`spikes/lang-explore/`, three seams in `spikes/minigit-bench/`, the bench ruleset data in `spikes/lang-bench/`)

*The commit shas named in this section are of the local working branch `feat/lang-v2-f3s1-prereg-tools`, which was never pushed. PR 5 is published as one commit on `feat/lang-v2-f3s1-prereg` because a test fixture in that history held a string the v1 leak check names (D41); the fixture was changed before the commit, and the tree is the one these runs tested.*

Nothing here pays, calls a model, pushes a tag or applies a ruleset. Every git test builds a temporary repository with a bare origin (`test/stage-prereg-fixture.mjs`; `test/stage-prereg-wiring-fixture.mjs` makes it complete: PR 4's invented record, a manifest, the registered prompts, a schedule with its pilot, the price file and the policy; its "tag" is annotated, pushed to the temporary origin and means nothing); the wiring tests then go through `stage-run` and `stage-init` with the fakes of the existing stage tests (the loop, the launch checks, PR 4's identities). The price tool is run with a fake `fetch`, never the network; the CLI pin with a temporary home; the seed verbs with a temporary home and tree. Nothing real is read or written, no held-out value is in any fixture.

```bash
LOW="node .agents/conventions/tools/low-priority.mjs taskset -c 0-5"
$LOW node --test --test-concurrency=1 spikes/lang-explore/test/stage-prereg.test.mjs        # the generators' pure parts, the contract, the prompts, the rule files, the leak check, the prices
$LOW node --test --test-concurrency=1 spikes/lang-explore/test/stage-prereg-tools.test.mjs  # the seed verbs, the manifest, the price tool, the CLI pin
$LOW node --test --test-concurrency=1 spikes/lang-explore/test/stage-prereg-guard.test.mjs  # the guard over the lists, the series' branch, stage-sync
$LOW node --test --test-concurrency=1 spikes/lang-explore/test/stage-prereg-wiring.test.mjs # the guard plugged into stage-run and stage-init
$LOW node --test --test-concurrency=1 spikes/lang-explore/test/code-shape.test.mjs          # lib/ AND prereg/: every function under 50 lines and a complexity of at most 4
$LOW node --test --test-concurrency=1 spikes/minigit-bench/test/prereg-guard.test.mjs spikes/minigit-bench/test/seed.test.mjs
$LOW node --test --test-concurrency=1 spikes/lang-bench/test/github-lang.test.mjs spikes/lang-bench/test/github-rulesets.test.mjs
```

**How the first RED was made.** The tests of 60 to 64, 66 to 68, 78 and 79f were written after the code, so a red from "no code" does not exist; each scenario below was proven by MUTATIONS of the code: one exact text of the code replaced, the test file run alone, the file restored byte for byte (`briefs/f3-pr5-red/` keeps the script and the log). Per scenario: the first mutation is the RED, then the restored code is the GREEN (the file's counts), and the other mutations are RED again. A mutation that no test caught would be printed as SURVIVED; none of the runs below survived. The two mutants the first draft left alive (the removal of the temporary sibling of a failed write, and the check that a rule leaves no key open) each have a test now (`A_write_that_cannot_replace_its_target_removes_the_sibling_it_wrote_and_leaves_the_target_alone`, `A_row_given_twice_is_refused_and_a_row_that_leaves_a_key_open_is_named_with_its_keys`), and the "extra rule" mutation is caught by a test that names it (`A_row_or_a_rule_in_the_file_that_the_contract_does_not_name_is_refused_by_its_id`). Scenarios 65 (the seed at the tag), 69 to 76 and 79a to 79d, 77 (v1's guard over v1's lists) and the bench ruleset data were proven red, green, red before the merge of `origin/main` (`briefs/f3-pr5-red/notes.md`: v1's guard 17 tests, 6 mutations; the seed 9 tests, 5; the rulesets 120 tests, 10; the guard over the lists 19 mutations); after the answers of the team lead changed the caps parser, `stage-sync` and the harness acceptance, 72, 75, 76, 79d and 79e were proven again below.

**Scenario map.** 60 `Every_quoted_block_of_the_contract_is_the_parents_text_...`; 61 `A_quote_whose_block_is_missing_...`; 62 `The_prompts_the_schedule_and_the_contract_are_written_again_to_the_same_bytes_...` and, for the manifest, `The_manifest_is_written_again_to_the_same_bytes_...` and its companions; 63 `The_registered_prompt_of_a_task_and_stack_is_the_text_the_engine_sends`; 64 `The_registered_statement_hashes_are_the_published_workspaces`; 65 and 65a `The_seed_is_in_the_tree_as_a_hash_only_...` and `The_real_draw_path_refuses_a_colliding_seed_...` (through the real seed verbs); 66 `No_registered_file_holds_a_case_id_...`; 67 `The_contract_states_the_claims_limit_...`; 68 `The_schedule_file_is_refused_without_the_pilots_tasks_...`; 69 to 76 in `stage-prereg-guard.test.mjs`; 77 in v1's `prereg-guard.test.mjs`; 78 `The_engine_prices_a_stage_session_from_the_registered_price_file` and the price tool's tests; 79 `A_stage_session_runs_the_pinned_CLI_copy_by_its_sha256`; 79a to 79e in `stage-prereg-guard.test.mjs` (79e is now `A_harness_fix_on_main_with_its_dated_line_is_taken_by_stage_sync_accept_harness_and_a_paid_launch_then_passes` and its companions of "PR 5 epoch 11: (b) restored"; it was, until epoch 11, `A_harness_acceptance_is_a_dated_line_that_stage_sync_takes_from_main_...` and `stage_sync_refuses_the_accept_harness_flag_...`); 79f the five `...rule...` tests of `stage-prereg.test.mjs`. The wiring tests of `stage-prereg-wiring.test.mjs` have no number in the brief (PR 4's point 10): the default guard of a paid launch (a control that passes every guard, one test per reason PR 5's guard refuses, each leaving the run-map and the ledger byte for byte as they were), a rehearsal still running with no tag, the default harness reader, the default `harnessAccepted` before and after the first data session, the one list of the policy's free fields, and `stage-init`'s default guard (a control that makes a paid series and records what the pre-registration holds, one test per reason it refuses, nothing written).

**Counts after the work** (each file alone, `taskset -c 0-5`, all green): `stage-prereg.test.mjs` 20, `stage-prereg-tools.test.mjs` 16, `stage-prereg-guard.test.mjs` 20, `stage-prereg-wiring.test.mjs` 23, `code-shape.test.mjs` 175 (it now measures `prereg/*.mjs` as well; a function of 5 in complexity and one of 50 lines in `prereg/` are each red: scenarios `shape a` and `shape b` above), `commands.test.mjs` 15, v1's `prereg-guard.test.mjs` 17 and `seed.test.mjs` 9, lang-bench's `github-lang.test.mjs` 81 and `github-rulesets.test.mjs` 39, and every other `stage-*.test.mjs` file as before (`stage-amendments` 9, `stage-archive` 29, `stage-commands` 12, `stage-disk` 12, `stage-facts` 3, `stage-guard-real` 2, `stage-guard` 19, `stage-heldout` 7, `stage-loop` 8, `stage-recovery-commands` 9, `stage-rounds` 16, `stage-run` 19, `stage-schedule` 53, `stage-series` 8, `stage-wiring` 10). The two tests that PR 3 and PR 4 own and that this work changed: `stage-run.test.mjs` `A_stage_series_never_runs_with_the_guard_that_always_passes` now removes the default pre-registration guard from the deps to show the refusal by name (PR 5 plugs its own in by default), and `stage-series.test.mjs` `stage_init_with_the_default_guard_refuses_a_paid_series_while_no_tag_is_on_the_remote_and_makes_a_rehearsal_series` runs the default init guard over a temporary repository.

**Covered.** The tools' pure parts and their disk edges; the guard over Stage 1's lists, one test per refusal and per permitted change; the series' branch and `stage-sync`; the default wiring into `stage-run` and `stage-init` through the fixtures above.

**Not covered, and why.** The real tag on the real remote, the real `gh` ruleset apply and the real pricing page: each is a step with the owner's go or a network read that a test must not make (the first real price read is PR 6's, and its values are compared with the page by the operator). The real manifest (`write-manifest.mjs` with no seam reads the real checkout, the real task repository, the real caches and the quick run's real series folders; only its pure parts and the guard's reading of its output are tested). `explore.mjs stage-sync` against a real clone (the verb is tested through `cmdStageSync` over a temporary repository with a bare origin). The dry run in a scratch clone is step T's, not a test.

### PR 5 coai plan round (2026-11-20, seven findings, same branch; each RED-GREEN-RED, one commit each, the `git log` of `feat/lang-v2-f3s1-prereg-tools` after `4e36eae0` names them)

For each finding: the test that went RED on the code of `4e36eae0` (pass/fail of its file and the first message), the GREEN count, and the RED-again with the old code put back as ONE mutation (the file or files of the fix replaced by their text at the commit before it). "Same" means the same tests failed with the same messages. Each file ran alone with `node .agents/conventions/tools/low-priority.mjs taskset -c 0-5 node --test --test-concurrency=1 <file>`.

- **S. the seed's partial states** (`minigit-bench/test/seed.test.mjs` +6, `stage-prereg-tools.test.mjs` +1; commit 14f6f0f0, shape split 1e65671c). Tests: `A_seed_file_with_no_hash_file_was_never_committed_so_the_retry_refuses_keeps_it_and_names_the_way_out` (and, after the operator removes the file, the retry draws a new seed), `A_hash_file_with_no_seed_file_is_a_lost_seed_and_the_commit_and_the_reveal_both_refuse_loudly`, `A_kill_while_the_hash_is_written_leaves_no_hash_file_and_no_temp_file_and_the_retry_names_the_seed_state`, `The_hash_file_is_written_by_a_rename_of_a_whole_temp_file_...`, `A_reveal_that_runs_again_on_the_same_seed_says_already_revealed_and_writes_nothing_and_a_different_seed_txt_is_refused`, `A_kill_while_seed_txt_is_written_leaves_no_seed_txt_and_the_reveal_runs_again`, and, over the verbs, `A_seed_commit_that_was_stopped_between_its_two_writes_is_named_by_the_verb_and_a_second_reveal_says_already_revealed`. RED (pass 9, fail 6): `The input did not match the regular expression /exists but .*seed\.sha256 does not: that seed was never committed and is not reused; ... : '<dir>/stage1.seed already exists: a seed is committed once'`, and for the lost seed `'<dir>/seed.sha256 already exists: the committed hash is frozen by the pre-registration tag'`. GREEN: seed.test.mjs pass 15, fail 0 (v1's 9 unchanged and green). RED again (`git show HEAD:spikes/minigit-bench/lib/seed.mjs`): pass 9, fail 6, the same six. The verb's test was written after the fix (it checks the wiring over the real home's path, which is a temporary home).
  - **The choice written down:** a seed file with no hash file is REFUSED, never moved aside. The tool cannot know why the hash is gone (a checkout that dropped it would make that file the committed seed), and it never deletes or renames a private seed it did not just make; the sentence names the way out (the operator removes the file, then runs seed-commit again).
  - **v1's "revealed once" stays a refusal value** (`ok: false`), so v1's test is unchanged; the second reveal of the SAME content says `already revealed: <file> holds the committed seed; nothing was written` and carries `alreadyRevealed: true`; other content is `seed.txt exists and differs from the committed seed: refused, nothing was written`.
- **P. the price read's timeout** (`stage-prereg-tools.test.mjs` +5; commit 0507d686). `A_fetch_that_never_answers_ends_by_its_timeout_as_a_refusal_that_names_it_and_the_request_carries_the_signal` (a fake fetch that never answers, `seams.timeoutMs: 20`), plus the four named scenarios the brief asks for, as tests of their own: `A_valid_price_table_is_parsed_into_one_price_file_with_each_models_own_row`, `A_header_that_is_not_the_exact_one_is_refused_and_no_file_is_written`, `A_model_of_the_base_file_with_no_row_on_the_page_is_refused_and_named`, `A_response_that_is_not_200_is_refused_with_its_status_and_no_file_is_written` (the four already held in older tests that bundle them; they passed on the old code). RED (pass 21, fail 1): `the pricing page cannot be read: Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'addEventListener')` against `the pricing page did not answer within 20 ms: the read timed out (https://platform.claude.com/docs/en/about-claude/pricing)`. GREEN pass 22. RED again (old read-prices.mjs): pass 21, fail 1, the same message. `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS` is 30000.
- **C. the CLI pin** (`stage-prereg-tools.test.mjs` +4; commit b4967cad). `A_kill_in_the_middle_of_the_copy_leaves_no_truncated_target_and_the_retry_pins_the_release` (a seam `installFn` that writes five bytes and throws; a stale read-only temp from a real kill is replaced), `The_same_file_already_pinned_is_reported_as_already_pinned_and_nothing_is_written_again` (same inodes of the copy and of explore.json), `Another_file_at_the_copys_place_is_refused_naming_both_hashes_and_a_read_only_file_is_never_overwritten`, `The_release_at_its_place_with_a_mode_other_than_0555_is_refused_not_rewritten`. v1's `installCli` is NOT atomic (`copyFileSync` straight to the target, then `chmod`): it is wrapped, run over a temp file of the target's folder and renamed into place; v1 is unchanged. RED (pass 22, fail 4): `Expected values to be strictly equal: actual: 0, expected: 5` (the killed copy pinned anyway), `actual: 'pinned CLI 2.1.330: ..., explore.json updated'` against `/^already pinned CLI .../`, `<copy> exists and is not the pinned CLI (sha256 differs)`. GREEN pass 26. RED again (old pin-cli.mjs): pass 22, fail 4, the same four.
- **H. a harness acceptance is frozen, never forgotten** (`stage-prereg-wiring.test.mjs`, +2 tests, 2 changed; commit 68ae90ab). `A_harness_accepted_by_a_dated_line_in_time_is_recorded_with_the_first_data_plan_and_the_three_next_launches_pass` (four launches across the boundary, read-back lines given), `A_dated_line_after_the_boundary_that_names_a_new_identity_is_refused_and_the_recorded_identity_stays_accepted`. The two older tests that said "never after the first data session" were changed to the new rule (the identity accepted in time stays accepted; a new line after the boundary accepts nothing). The record is `harnessAtFirstData` (12 hex) in the run-map the first data plan writes (`planUnit`, PR 3's one `commitPlan` write; no second write); `stageGuard` now answers `ok({ harness })` so the plan records the identity the guard just compared. RED (pass 22, fail 3): `the identity the guard accepted is in the run-map the plan wrote: actual: undefined, expected: 'eeeeeeeeeeee'`, `the recorded identity is still accepted: actual: 4, expected: 0`. GREEN pass 25. RED again (`acceptedHarness` no longer reads the record): pass 22, fail 3: `launch 2 across the boundary: 1 problem(s) before a paid launch: harness differs: the manifest has ea847efa3e58, this checkout has eeeeeeeeeeee`.

RED again (the flag is ignored): pass 26, fail 2 (the no-flag refusal passes both ways, as it should).
- **R. the price page snapshot** (`stage-prereg-tools.test.mjs` +3, 2 changed to pass `--base`; commit 79d9092a). `The_fetched_page_is_saved_beside_the_price_file_with_its_url_and_time_and_passes_the_leak_tiers` (the D41 tiers `scanFiles` over the snapshot on a fixture, clean and, with a case line added, found), `The_check_regenerates_from_the_snapshot_and_makes_no_network_call`, `An_edited_snapshot_an_edited_price_file_and_a_missing_snapshot_each_fail_the_check_with_a_sentence`. RED again (old read-prices.mjs and generator.mjs): pass 27, fail 2, the same.
- **F. a failed fetch is a named refusal** (`stage-prereg-guard.test.mjs` +2; commit d1ddfaf2). v1's `git()` already answers a non-zero exit as `fail` and `branchProblems` and `stageSync` already refuse on it, so `A_fetch_that_exits_non_zero_refuses_the_guard_and_stage_sync_naming_the_fetch` passed on the old code (it is the regression test). The gap was the timeout: 60 s fixed, answered as `git fetch exited null`. `A_fetch_that_does_not_end_is_stopped_by_its_timeout_...` (a remote `ext::sleep 5`, `fetchMs: 300`). GREEN pass 22. RED again: pass 21, fail 1, the same. `FETCH_MS` is 120000 (`stage-branch.mjs`); v1's `git(repo, args, { timeoutMs })` names a timeout.

### PR 5 Opus review (2026-11-20, eleven findings, same branch; same method)

- **V1. the engine sends the registered prompt** (`stage-prereg-wiring.test.mjs` +3; commit 9348db19). `The_prompt_plan_gets_for_a_unit_of_a_paid_series_is_the_registered_text_byte_for_byte` (`seriesPrompts`, the call of `run.mjs:140`), `A_rehearsal_slice_holds_the_stage_wording_the_engine_sends_and_not_the_quick_runs`, `A_slice_holding_another_text_than_the_registered_one_is_refused_at_launch_naming_the_task_and_stack`. **Decision:** a paid series' slices hold the REGISTERED texts of `prompts-lang.json`; a rehearsal's slice holds `stagePrompts` (the same Stage 1 wording, no registered file), so a rehearsal rehearses the real wording. ### PR 5 epoch 11: (b) restored (2026-11-20, same branch; same method: tests first, RED on the current code, fix, GREEN, the old code back as one mutation per point, RED again, fix restored)

The re-review of the whole plan (epoch 11, pull request #240) sent candidate (b) back to the brief: `stage-sync --accept-harness <12 hex>` takes the harness folders of the commit that a dated line names. Commit 4c07bc68. Each file alone, `low-priority.mjs taskset -c 0-5 node --test --test-concurrency=1`. The code is `lib/stage-accept.mjs` (new; reads the line through PR 3's `parseStageAmendments`, reuses `stage-branch.mjs`'s `fetched`, `refusal` and `restored`) and `cmdStageSync` (the series locks D3, the in-memory plan and restore D4, the `fetching` line D5 and the fetch timeout F are the ones `stage-sync` has).

RED on the current code (guard pass 36, fail 13 of 49; wiring pass 31, fail 1 of 32). Every red test met the old refusal, which is what the code did:

- the happy path `A_harness_fix_on_main_with_its_dated_line_is_taken_by_stage_sync_accept_harness_and_a_paid_launch_then_passes`: `--accept-harness is not a flag of stage-sync: a harness acceptance is a dated line ... never writes an acceptance itself`, `4 !== 0`.
- the six cases below, and the two sentences of the guard (`A_harness_identity_that_is_not_the_series_refuses_...` in the guard tests and `the harness identity is not the series'` in the wiring tests): `The input did not match the regular expression /harnessIdentity [0-9a-f]{12} is not the run-map's [0-9a-f]{12} and no amendment accepted it \(put the fix on origin\/main, add the dated line ...` against `... no amendment accepted it (add the dated line `YYYY-MM-DD | instrument | harness:<12 hex> | <reason, with its G-id>` to .../amendments.md on origin/main, then stage-sync)`.
- the bare flag: `actual: '--accept-harness is not a flag of stage-sync: ...'` against `expected: /^--accept-harness needs a value: the 12 hex \(0-9, a-f\) of the harness identity ... as in `stage-sync --accept-harness 0123456789ab`/`.
- the other 11 new tests fail the same way (code 4 and the old sentence where the new one is expected); `stage_sync_without_the_flag_takes_the_registered_files_only_and_never_a_harness_folder` passes on the old code (a regression test).

GREEN: `stage-prereg-guard` pass 49, fail 0; `stage-prereg-wiring` pass 32, fail 0.

The six cases of the brief, and the old code back as one mutation per point (each mutation is a one-line change of the new code; the file is restored after it; each run is the guard file alone, pass 48, fail 1 of 49 unless noted):

1. **The happy path** (a fix commit on main that edits one harness file, deletes one and adds one, with a note outside the harness; the dated line with `commit:<sha>` on main after it; `stage-sync` takes the line; `stage-sync --accept-harness <hex>`). The branch holds exactly those three harness files in the tree and in ONE commit (the note stays the tag's), its identity is the fix's, the tree is clean, nothing is pushed, a paid launch passes the guard with the identity accepted and is refused with the new sentence without it, a second call says `nothing to take`. The mutation of the whole point, the old refusal back (`callProblem` refuses the flag): pass 37, fail 12, the first being `--accept-harness is not a flag of stage-sync ...`, `4 !== 0`.
2. **A commit not reachable from `origin/main`** (the fix pushed to another branch of the origin) is refused: `commit <12 hex> is not reachable from origin/main: the fix must be merged on main first (a public hash identifies bytes, it does not supply the reviewed source), and the series takes it from there`; the tree is as it was. Mutation (the reachability test answers `null`): `stage-sync: fetching origin ...|stage-sync --accept-harness 8bfe1ef05284: committed f7adaead025a: 3 harness files taken from commit ae9f49f4eb19`, `0 !== 4`.
3. **A line with no `commit:` token, a token that does not begin the reason, a short sha and two tokens** (four tests) are refused: `the dated line for harness:<hex> does not begin its reason with `commit:<40 hex>`: stage-sync reads that token only ...` and `... holds more than one `commit:` token: it names one commit`; also no line for the identity (`no dated line `YYYY-MM-DD | instrument | harness:ffffffffffff | commit:<40 hex> <reason>` in ... on origin/main`) and two lines for one identity (`2 dated lines name harness:<hex>: one line names one commit`). Mutations: the pattern not anchored (`/commit:...` for `/^commit:...`): the not-the-beginning test, `... committed c73ffaccc4a3: 3 harness files taken ...`, `0 !== 4`; `{7,40}` for `{40}`: the short-sha test, `0 !== 4`; the second-token test answering `true`: the two-token test, `0 !== 4`.
4. **A taken identity that is not the 12 hex** (the line names `aaaaaaaaaaaa`, the folders of the commit have another identity) is refused, and the tree is restored: `the harness folders of commit <12 hex> have the identity <12 hex>, not the aaaaaaaaaaaa the dated line names: nothing was taken: every file it had taken is restored as HEAD has it, and nothing is staged`; the branch, the index and the tree are as they were (the deleted file is back, the added one gone). Mutation of the check (`true ? null :`): `... committed ac626394ac27: 3 harness files taken from commit 6cf11371126f`, `0 !== 4`. Mutation of the restore (`fail(problem)` for `putBack(...)`): `the files the take had written and staged are back as the branch had them`, actual `A  spikes/lang-bench/tools/new.mjs`, `D  spikes/lang-explore/lib/old.mjs`, `M  spikes/lang-explore/lib/x.mjs` staged and the diff of the tree.
5. **Once a data session has left `planned`** the command is refused (`a data session has left planned in the series <dir>: the instrument is frozen with the first data session, so no harness is accepted now: start a new series`); so it is once the first data plan froze the acceptance into the run-map (`the first data plan of the series <dir> froze the harness identity <hex>: no other harness is accepted now`; the guard would refuse the new identity then), the locks are released and the tree is as it was; a series with no data session accepts (the control). 6. **A bare `--accept-harness`** (and `=`, an upper-case value, 11 hex, and a last value that is not hex) is refused before any fetch with one sentence: `--accept-harness needs a value: the 12 hex (0-9, a-f) of the harness identity that a dated line `YYYY-MM-DD | instrument | harness:<12 hex> | commit:<40 hex> <reason>` names in inputs/prereg/amendments.md on origin/main, as in `stage-sync --accept-harness 0123456789ab` (got ...)`. Mutation (the value check removed): `stage-sync --accept-harness: refused before any fetch: stage-sync: fetching origin ...|no dated line ... harness: | commit:<40 hex> ...`, `2 !== 1`. (The first run of this mutation ran on the real repository, because the test passed no repository: it was stopped by `the tree is not clean`, by luck. The test now passes a temporary repository and a temporary home, so no mutation of the check reaches a real remote.)

Three more tests, each of a thing the brief names without a case:

One mutation found no test and none is written: the commit's pathspec (`'-a'` for `'--', ...staged`): `stage-sync` refuses a staged or changed file anywhere before it takes anything, so nothing but the taken paths can be staged and the pathspec is a second guard; it stays as the brief and D5's `commitTaken` have it.

- **E2 and E3. a kill in the middle of a take, and a lookup that fails** (`test/stage-prereg-take.test.mjs`, 9 new tests; `test/stage-take-child.mjs`, the child they run; commit 27f4a44f). Every kill is a real SIGKILL: a child process runs `acceptHarness` with a seam (`seam(step)`) that kills the child at the named step (`indexBuilt`, `journalWritten`, `refMoved`, `worktree:<n>` before each step of the working-tree update, `worktreeDone`), so no rollback and no `finally` of the dying process can run. A failed lookup is a `git` of the test's own, first on the child's PATH, that fails the one call it is told to (`exit 128`, `fatal: simulated lookup failure`) and runs the real one for every other. Nothing is real: every repository is temporary with a bare origin.
  1. `A_process_killed_at_indexBuilt_...` and `..._at_journalWritten_...` (2 tests): the branch, the index and the tree are the old ones; the next plain `stage-sync` is not refused, takes nothing and drops the journal; a retry of `--accept-harness` then takes (one commit, clean tree, the harness identity of the fix, no journal and no index file).
  2. `A_process_killed_after_the_branch_moved_leaves_a_state_the_guard_names_and_the_next_stage_sync_finishes_at_every_step`: kills at `refMoved`, `worktree:0`, `worktree:1` and `worktreeDone`. After each: the branch holds the new commit, the paid guard refuses naming `an interrupted harness take is recorded in .../stage-take.journal`, the next plain `stage-sync` finishes the take (the same end state as above) and a retry says nothing to take.
  3. `A_process_killed_in_the_middle_of_the_working_tree_update_is_finished_by_the_retry_of_the_same_command`: after the kill the tree is dirty (the old clean-tree check alone would refuse the retry); the retry of `--accept-harness` finishes it and commits nothing more.
  4. `A_kill_during_the_move_of_the_shared_rules_checkout_is_finished_and_the_submodule_ends_at_the_new_commit`: the same four kill points over a fix that moves the `.agents/conventions` gitlink; after the next `stage-sync` the branch records the new rules commit, the submodule is checked out there and the status is clean; `worktree:2` is no kill point (the list of points is whole).
  5. `A_branch_that_moves_while_the_take_is_built_is_refused_by_the_compare_and_swap_and_nothing_else_changes`: a commit of somebody else lands between the journal and `update-ref`; the take is refused (`the branch refs/heads/series is no longer at <12 hex>: nothing was taken`), the branch is where the other commit put it, the tree is the old one, clean, the journal dropped; a retry takes on top.
  6. `A_journal_that_cannot_be_read_or_a_branch_that_moved_on_stops_stage_sync_naming_it_and_changes_nothing`: a journal that is not JSON (`stage-take.journal cannot be read`) and a journal whose commits the branch is not at (`the branch refs/heads/series is at <12 hex>, which is neither aaaaaaaaaaaa nor bbbbbbbbbbbb`) each stop `stage-sync` by name; nothing is dropped or written.
  7. E3, `A_refused_identity_with_a_failing_lookup_of_the_paths_of_HEAD_removes_no_file_because_no_file_was_written`: the dated line names an identity the fix does not have, and the child's `git` fails `ls-tree -r --name-only` (the old rollback's lookup); the refusal is the identity sentence and the tree, the index and the branch are as they were (`x.mjs` is still there).
  8. E3, `A_failed_lookup_of_either_tree_while_a_take_is_finished_refuses_naming_the_failure_and_removes_no_file`: killed at `refMoved`, then `stage-sync` with a `git` that fails the `ls-tree` of the old commit, and again of the new one: the refusal is `git ls-tree exited 128: fatal: simulated lookup failure: the harness entries of the interrupted take cannot be read, so no file was removed or written`, `old.mjs` is still in the tree, the status is as it was, the journal is kept; with the real `git` the next `stage-sync` finishes the take.

  RED on the current code (the file's 9 tests, pass 0, fail 9; the first run was before two corrections of the tests themselves, the same 9 failed on the final tests, run against the whole old `lib/`): the old `acceptHarness` has no seam, so it ran to its end and the child was not killed: `the child was to die at indexBuilt:` (`false !== true`) and the same at `journalWritten`, `refMoved` and `worktree:1`; the retry and the branch tests failed on the same assertions or on `Expected values to be strictly equal`. E3 failed with the real defect: in `A_refused_identity_with_a_failing_lookup_...` `Error: ENOENT: no such file or directory, open '<repo>/spikes/lang-explore/lib/x.mjs'`, that is, the rollback, with its second lookup failed, took every staged path for absent from HEAD and removed `x.mjs`, a file HEAD holds. (The old code cannot be killed at a step, so its E2 red is the missing seam; the original defect itself, a partly replaced harness and a refused retry, is what tests 2 and 3 describe for any code that edits the tree before it moves the branch.)

  GREEN: `stage-prereg-take` pass 9, fail 0; the other files of the list unchanged (counts below).

  RED again, the old code back as one mutation per finding (the file is restored after each; each run is the new file alone):
  - E2: the whole old `lib/stage-accept.mjs` put back over the new one (`git show 196087fa:...`): pass 0, fail 9, the same messages as the first RED (`the child was to die at indexBuilt:`, ..., `ENOENT ... x.mjs` for E3).
  - E3: a lookup that fails taken for an empty tree (`if (unread) return ok(new Map());` for `return unread` in `entriesAt` of `lib/stage-take.mjs`): pass 8, fail 1, `A_failed_lookup_of_either_tree_while_a_take_is_finished_refuses_naming_the_failure_and_removes_no_file`: message `old`, actual `the tree is not clean: spikes/lang-explore/lib/old.mjs is staged or changed: stage-sync commits only what it takes from origin/main, ...`, expected `/git ls-tree exited 128: fatal: simulated lookup failure: the harness entries of the interrupted take cannot be read, so no file was removed or written/` (the finish went on with an empty tree and left the take half done instead of refusing).

  Not tested, said here: the compare-and-swap test makes the branch move through the seam, in the same process; a real race between two processes is covered by the series locks that `cmdStageSync` holds, not by a test. The commit of an accept-harness take is made by `commit-tree`, so a pre-commit hook no longer runs for it (plain `stage-sync` still commits with `git commit`, and its D4 hook test is unchanged).

1. **Only the series' own branch.** `stage-init` records the branch HEAD is on in the series state (`branch`, `stageInitGuard` through `recorded`; a detached head is refused; a rehearsal records none). Both forms of `stage-sync` refuse unless HEAD is a branch a paid Stage 1 series of the explore root records AND that series' tag commit is in its history (`git merge-base --is-ancestor`): `the branch feature is not the series' own branch: a Stage 1 series records the-series-branch: stage-sync moves only that branch, so change to it`; with no series, `no Stage 1 series is made under the explore root: there is nothing to sync for`; `the tag's commit <12 hex> of the series is not in the history of the branch <b>`. Tests: `A_clean_feature_branch_that_the_series_does_not_name_...` (both forms, nothing moves), `The_series_own_branch_passes_and_a_series_that_records_no_branch_in_the_history_of_HEAD_or_no_series_at_all_is_refused`, and the wiring test of `stage-init` asserts `state.branch`. Without a `home` (a test seam, never in `explore.mjs`) no series is known and only the old rule (not main, not detached) applies. Mutation (`return null` for the series check): pass 0, fail 2, `plain: stage-sync: fetching origin ...|stage-sync: committed fe0ed99063e2: .../seed.txt`.
2. **`origin/main` by its full name.** `MAIN` is `refs/remotes/origin/main`: the reachability test, the amendments read, the blob checks and the sync takes all use it. Test: `A_local_branch_named_origin_main_...` (a local branch of that name at an unmerged commit): refused, `commit <12 hex> is not reachable from origin/main`. Mutation (`'origin/main'` back): pass 0, fail 1, the same sentence not matched.
3. **Every series is locked and freeze-checked.** (a) A series of the explore root whose state cannot be read refuses both forms (`unreadableSeriesDirs`): `the series <dir> cannot be read (...), so stage-sync cannot be told whether a unit runs or a data session has left planned`. (b) With `--series X` every Stage 1 series is freeze-checked (the locks of all were already taken) and an X that is not under the root is refused: `<dir> is not a series under the explore root of this home`. Tests: `A_series_whose_state_cannot_be_read_...`, `With_a_series_flag_every_Stage_1_series_is_freeze_checked_and_locked_and_a_series_outside_the_root_is_refused`. Mutations: no unreadable check: pass 0, fail 1 (`plain: ... nothing to take`); freeze check over the first series only: pass 0, fail 1 (`the series flag is not given: the frozen one is found`... for the named case, `a data session has left planned in the series <second>` not matched); root check removed: pass 0, fail 1.
4. **A test that could not fail.** `failingGit('ls-tree -r --name-only')` matched no call the code makes. The test is now `A_failed_lookup_of_the_harness_entries_refuses_the_take_naming_it_and_writes_nothing`, over the happy fixture, with the shim failing `ls-tree -r -z` (the call of `entriesAt`). Mutation (the earlier E3 one, `if (unread) return ok(new Map())`): pass 0, fail 1, `The input did not match the regular expression /git ls-tree exited 128: fatal: simulated lookup failure/`.
5. **Removals are values and look first.** `gone(file)` (exported) wraps every removal (`dropped`, `readiedIndex`, the unlink of a removed file, the temporary index of the accept): `cannot remove <path>: <error>`. Before step 4 writes anything a change is checked: a link on the way to any changed path (`linkOnWay` of `lib/safe-place.mjs`, nothing is followed) and, among the removed paths, a folder: `<link> is a link on the way to <file>, a path of the take: nothing is written or removed through a link`, `<file> is a folder, not a file: it is not removed`; the journal is kept. Tests: `A_removed_path_that_is_now_a_folder_...`, `A_parent_folder_that_is_a_link_...`, `A_removal_that_fails_is_a_refusal_value_naming_the_path_and_the_error_never_a_throw`. The recovery and the first run's step 4 refuse when a changed path's working file is neither the old nor the new blob (`git hash-object`): `the working file <path> is neither the old nor the new blob of the interrupted take: a person edited it: nothing was written`. Tests: `A_file_edited_between_the_build_and_the_working_tree_step_...` (through the seam `indexBuilt`), `A_working_file_that_is_neither_the_old_nor_the_new_blob_...`. ### PR 5b: read-prices meets the real page (2026-11-20, branch `fix/lang-v2-read-prices`, cut from main 9e930582; same method: tests first, RED on the current code, fix, GREEN, the old behaviour back as one mutation per point, RED again, fix restored)

The first real read of the pricing page (fetched 2026-11-20) was a refusal. Two gaps: `PRICE_HEADER` was written before anyone had seen the page (`Base Input Tokens ... Cache Hits & Refreshes`; the real header is `Base input tokens ... Cache hits and refreshes`), and some price cells end with a footnote mark (`$0.20 / MTok<sup>1</sup>`). A third gap showed in the real page: the name cells with a note in parentheses (`Claude Haiku 5.5 (for prompts up to 100,000 tokens)` and `(... over 100,000 tokens)`) lost the note, so the two rows had the same name and different prices, and the whole table was refused as a conflict. Decision of the team lead (plan point Q6: exact, never guesses). Each file alone, `low-priority.mjs taskset -c 0-5 node --test --test-concurrency=1`. Commits: tests and fixture 78986aca (red), code 92a3f56e.

The fixture is `test/fixtures/pricing-page-2026-11-20.excerpt.md`: an excerpt of the real page (the price table and its footnote lines byte for byte, plus the fast-mode table, which has another header, so the choice of the right table is tested). Public text; its first line says where and when it was taken. The new tests are in `test/read-prices-real-page.test.mjs` (8 tests; no network).

RED on the code of PR 5: `read-prices-real-page.test.mjs`, pass 0, fail 8 of 8. The first assertion of each: (1) the header, `Expected values to be strictly deep-equal` (actual `Base Input Tokens`, `5m Cache Writes`, ... , expected the real header); (2) to (5) `TypeError: Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'prices')` (the parse of the real page is a refusal, so there is no page value); (6) `AssertionError: the previous header` (the old code still read the old header); (7) and (8) `the pricing page has no table with the exact header row | Model | Base Input Tokens | 5m Cache Writes | 1h Cache Writes | Cache Hits & Refreshes | Output Tokens |: it holds the tables | Model | Base input tokens | 5m cache writes ...` (the real page's table was listed as found and not read).

The fix (`prereg/read-prices.mjs`):

1. **The real header.** `PRICE_HEADER` is exactly `Model | Base input tokens | 5m cache writes | 1h cache writes | Cache hits and refreshes | Output tokens`; the match is the same exact cell comparison after the trim the parser already does. No case folding, no "&" for "and".
2. **One footnote mark.** A price cell is `$<number> / MTok`, optionally followed directly by exactly one `<sup><digits></sup>`, and nothing else. `rowOf` records the marks per rate (`marks`); the number in the cell is the price. Any other trailing text, a second mark, a space before the mark, a non-digit mark and an empty mark are refused naming the cell (`Cache hits and refreshes is '<cell>', not a price in the form $5 / MTok`).
3. **The mark is recorded with its sentence.** `parsePricingPage` reads the page's `*<sup>N sentence</sup>*` lines (`footnotes`); the price file's `notes` get one line per footnote the base models carry: `footnote 2 (claude-opus-5-5 cacheRead, claude-sonnet-5-5 cacheRead): Cache hits and refreshes on Claude Opus 5.5 and Claude Sonnet 5.5 are priced at 0.05x the base input price.` A mark of a base model whose sentence is not on the page is a refusal: `claude-opus-5-5 (Claude Opus 5.5): its cacheRead carries footnote mark 2 and the page has no footnote 2`. A footnote number given twice with different sentences is a refusal. The footnote of a model the base file does not use (footnote 3, Sonnet 5) is not recorded.
4. **Rows with a note in the name.** `plainName` no longer cuts the parenthesis off. A name with `(` is a row with a note: it is left unread (`unreadable`, why `its name carries a note, so the row is not read`), is not compared for conflicts and never breaks the table; it is named, with its note, in the "rows it found" list of a refusal. Only a base model with no clean row is a refusal.
5. The five base models read exactly the base file's figures from the real page (Opus 5.5 `4 / 20 / 0.2 / 5 / 8`); a run of the tool on the whole real page (`--from-file`, no network) wrote the file, and `--check` of it passed.

Tests adjusted in `stage-prereg-tools.test.mjs` (six; they encoded the old header or the old reading of a row with a note; nothing was weakened):
- `A_model_id_is_named_the_way_the_pricing_page_names_it_and_the_page_is_read_by_its_exact_header`: the row `Claude Old 1 (deprecated)` is now left unread with the note sentence, and the same row without the note still names its cell (`5m cache writes is 'n/a' ...`);
- `A_page_without_the_exact_header_row_is_refused_naming_the_headers_it_found_and_nothing_is_guessed`: the header sentence and the variants use the real header;
- `A_price_cell_is_read_only_in_the_form_dollars_per_MTok_and_a_name_given_twice_with_different_content_is_refused`: `Base input tokens is '4 USD' ...`;
- `The_price_file_has_the_base_files_models_each_from_its_own_row_and_never_an_older_price`: the "rows it found" list names `Claude Old 1 (deprecated)`;
- `A_pricing_page_that_changed_its_header_writes_no_file_and_exits_5_naming_what_it_found` and `A_header_that_is_not_the_exact_one_is_refused_and_no_file_is_written`: the text they change is the real header's (`Output tokens`, `Cache hits and refreshes`).

GREEN: `read-prices-real-page.test.mjs` 8/8, `stage-prereg-tools.test.mjs` 33/33, `code-shape.test.mjs` 177/177 (the complexity of every new function is at most 4), `network.test.mjs` 3/3.

The old behaviour back, one mutation per point (code of 92a3f56e with one edit; the two files `read-prices-real-page` and `stage-prereg-tools` together, 41 tests), each RED, then the fix restored:

| Mutation | Result | First message |
|---|---|---|
| case folding (`c.toLowerCase() === b[i].toLowerCase()` in `sameCells`) | pass 39, fail 2 | `the real header folded to lower case` (`true !== false`) and, in the old file, `lower case` |
| "&" for "and" (`c.replace(' & ', ' and ')` in `sameCells`) | pass 40, fail 1 | `an ampersand for "and"` (`true !== false`) |
| a second mark allowed (`(?:<sup>(\d+)<\/sup>){0,2}`) | pass 40, fail 1 | `$0.20 / MTok<sup>2</sup><sup>1</sup>` (`true !== false`) |
| the mark dropped silently (`marks: {}`) | pass 38, fail 3 | `Expected values to be strictly equal` `0 !== 2` (no footnote notes), `true !== false` (a mark with no sentence not refused) and the byte test |
| a mark with no sentence not refused (`markProblem` always null) | pass 40, fail 1 | `A_mark_whose_footnote_sentence_is_not_on_the_page_is_refused_...`: `true !== false` |
| a row with a note read (the `(` test off) | pass 39, fail 2 | `Rows_with_a_note_in_the_name_never_break_the_read_...`: `+ undefined - 'its name carries a note, so the row is not read'`, and the adjusted test of `stage-prereg-tools` |

Counts after the change (each file alone, all green): `read-prices-real-page.test.mjs` 8 (new), `stage-prereg-tools.test.mjs` 33, `code-shape.test.mjs` 177, `network.test.mjs` 3.

**PR 5b, coai plan round (two findings accepted; commits f09db2b8 tests (red) and the fix commit after it; same method).**
- *N1, a noted row that is a tier of a base model.* Before, any row with `(` in its name was skipped without a check, so a page with a clean `Claude Opus 5.5` row and a noted `Claude Opus 5.5 (for prompts over 100,000 tokens)` row at other prices would use the clean row: a guess. Now `tierProblem` takes each noted row's name before ` (`; when it is the display name of a base model, `buildPrices` refuses: `claude-opus-5-5 (Claude Opus 5.5): the page also gives the row 'Claude Opus 5.5 (for prompts over 100,000 tokens)': the pricing is tiered, so no price is chosen`. Noted rows of other models stay unread. A base model that has only noted rows of its own name is a tiered refusal too (my earlier PR 5b test expected `no row` for it; it now expects the tiered sentence, and a base model with no row at all still gets `no row (the rows it found: ...)`).
- *N2, the footnote syntax.* The constant `FOOTNOTE` (comment above it) is the one accepted form: a whole trimmed line `*<sup>N sentence</sup>*`, the number, one space, the sentence on one line. Skipped as not footnotes: a list item, a bullet, a table line, a bare `<sup>`, and the unnumbered `*<sup>All other models use the standard 0.1x multiplier.</sup>*`, which is NOT recorded (it covers the unmarked rates, and the file never guesses which). A line that starts like a footnote (`*<sup>` and a digit) and is not in the form (the sentence on the next line, text after the closing, no space after the number) is a refusal: `the pricing page has a footnote line that is not `*<sup>N sentence</sup>*` on one line: <line>`; a number with two different sentences is a refusal (unchanged); the same sentence twice is one footnote.
- New tests (5, in `read-prices-real-page.test.mjs`): the tier refusal; the noted row of a non-base model still passes; the EXACT `notes` of the real-page excerpt compared as strings (the generated line and the two footnote lines); a list item, a bullet and a bare sup are not footnotes (notes unchanged) and a duplicate number with another sentence is refused (`the pricing page gives footnote 2 twice with different sentences: ...`); a footnote spanning two lines, with text after the closing, or with no space after the number is refused naming the line.
- RED on the code of 84f95775: `read-prices-real-page.test.mjs` 13 tests, pass 11, fail 2: the tier test `true !== false` (the table was not refused) and the spanning-line test `true !== false`. The other three new tests pin behaviour that already held (the exact notes, the non-base noted row, the duplicate number) and were green at once; they are the guard of the new code.
- GREEN: `read-prices-real-page.test.mjs` 13/13, `stage-prereg-tools.test.mjs` 33/33, `code-shape.test.mjs` 177/177 (a first version of `modelProblem` had complexity 5 and was split: `absentProblem`, `tierProblem`), `network.test.mjs` 3/3.
- The old behaviour back, one mutation each (both test files, 46 tests): the tier check off: pass 44, fail 2 (`true !== false` in the tier test; the tiered sentence in the noted-rows test); the spanning check off (`if (false) return`): pass 45, fail 1 (`true !== false`, spanning test); the duplicate-number refusal off: pass 45, fail 1 (`true !== false`); list items taken as footnotes (`FOOTNOTE` loosened): the footnote-form tests fail (2 of 13 in the new file). Each fix restored after.
- **PR 5b, coai code round (two findings accepted; commits c73b73a0 test (red), the fix commit after it, and this docs commit).** *C1:* the footnote numbers are sorted with `toSorted((a, b) => a - b)`, not the mutating `sort` (convention fix; no red test, the existing tests stay green). *C2:* a tier row is found by the part of the name before the FIRST `(`, its whitespace collapsed and trimmed, compared EXACTLY with the base model's display name (only names that have a `(`, so an unreadable clean row is not a tier). New test `A_tier_row_with_two_spaces_or_no_space_before_the_parenthesis_...` (two spaces, no space, a tab and spaces: each refused as tiered; `Claude Opus 5.55 (...)` is not a tier). RED on the code before the fix: `read-prices-real-page.test.mjs` 14 tests, pass 13, fail 1: `AssertionError: two spaces`, `true !== false` (the clean row was used). A first version of the fix, without the `(` test, also failed 2 old tests (`... the pricing is tiered` named an unreadable clean row), and was corrected. GREEN: `read-prices-real-page.test.mjs` 14/14, `stage-prereg-tools.test.mjs` 33/33, `code-shape.test.mjs` 177/177, `network.test.mjs` 3/3. Mutation (the old prefix check `name.startsWith(\`${wanted.name} (\`)` back, both test files, 47 tests): pass 46, fail 1, `AssertionError: two spaces`, `true !== false`; the fix restored.

Why: the language comparison's pilot row G-4 has v1's B-T1 over a committed smoke fixture of the PINNED CLI as its replacing evidence, and the only fixture is v1's, on CLI 2.1.310; Stage 1 pins 2.1.329. v1's smoke took its CLI pin from `inputs/stacks.json` for the preflight, the plan and `meta.json`, so it could not capture a fixture on 2.1.329.
